===== ./src/App.jsx =====
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';

import RequireRole from './components/common/RequireRole';
import RedirectStaffToPortal from './components/common/RedirectStaffToPortal';

import PublicLayout from './layouts/PublicLayout';
import DoctorLayout from './layouts/DoctorLayout';
import ReceptionLayout from './layouts/ReceptionLayout';
import AdminLayout from './layouts/AdminLayout';

import HomePage from './pages/public/HomePage';
import DepartmentsPage from './pages/public/DepartmentsPage';
import DoctorsPage from './pages/public/DoctorsPage';
import DoctorDetailPage from './pages/public/DoctorDetailPage';
import AppointmentsPage from './pages/patient/AppointmentsPage';
import MedicalServicesPage from './pages/public/MedicalServicesPage';
import LoginPage from './pages/auth/LoginPage';
import RegisterPage from './pages/auth/RegisterPage';

import MyAppointmentsPage from './pages/patient/MyAppointmentsPage';
import MyExamReportsPage from './pages/patient/MyExamReportsPage';
import MyPaymentsPage from './pages/patient/MyPaymentsPage';
import ProfilePage from './pages/account/ProfilePage';

import DoctorDashboardPage from './pages/doctor/DoctorDashboardPage';
import DoctorAppointmentsPage from './pages/doctor/DoctorAppointmentsPage';
import DoctorSchedulePage from './pages/doctor/DoctorSchedulePage';
import DoctorExamReportsPage from './pages/doctor/DoctorExamReportsPage';

import ReceptionAppointmentsPage from './pages/reception/ReceptionAppointmentsPage';
import ReceptionPatientsPage from './pages/reception/ReceptionPatientsPage';
import ReceptionBookingPage from './pages/reception/ReceptionBookingPage';
import ReceptionBillingPage from './pages/reception/ReceptionBillingPage';
import ReceptionBillingDetailPage from './pages/reception/ReceptionBillingDetailPage';

import AdminDashboardPage from './pages/admin/AdminDashboardPage';
import AdminSystemConfigPage from './pages/admin/AdminSystemConfigPage';
import AdminUsersPage from './pages/admin/AdminUsersPage';
import AdminExamReportsPage from './pages/admin/AdminExamReportsPage';
import AdminWorkingHoursPage from './pages/admin/AdminWorkingHoursPage.jsx';
import AdminDoctorsPage from './pages/admin/AdminDoctorsPage';
import AdminDepartmentsPage from './pages/admin/AdminDepartmentsPage';
import AdminMedicalServicesPage from './pages/admin/AdminMedicalServicesPage';

import './App.css';

function App() {
    return (
        <BrowserRouter>
            <Routes>
                <Route element={<RedirectStaffToPortal />}>
                    <Route element={<PublicLayout />}>
                        <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
                        <Route path="/departments" element={<DepartmentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/doctors" element={<DoctorsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/doctors/:uuid" element={<DoctorDetailPage />} />
                        <Route path="/appointments" element={<AppointmentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/medical-services" element={<MedicalServicesPage />} />
                        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
                        <Route path="/register" element={<RegisterPage />} />
                    </Route>
                </Route>
                <Route element={<RequireRole allowedRoles={[1]} />}>
                    <Route element={<PublicLayout />}>
                        <Route path="/my-appointments" element={<MyAppointmentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/my-exam-reports" element={<MyExamReportsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/my-payments" element={<MyPaymentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
                    </Route>
                </Route>
                <Route element={<RequireRole allowedRoles={[2]} />}>
                    <Route element={<DoctorLayout />}>
                        <Route path="/doctor/dashboard" element={<DoctorDashboardPage />} />
                        <Route path="/doctor/appointments" element={<DoctorAppointmentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/doctor/schedule" element={<DoctorSchedulePage />} />
                        <Route path="/doctor/exam-reports" element={<DoctorExamReportsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/doctor/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
                    </Route>
                </Route>
                <Route element={<RequireRole allowedRoles={[3]} />}>
                    <Route element={<ReceptionLayout />}>
                        <Route path="/reception/appointments" element={<ReceptionAppointmentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/reception/patients" element={<ReceptionPatientsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/reception/booking" element={<ReceptionBookingPage />} />
                        <Route path="/reception/billing" element={<ReceptionBillingPage />} />
                        <Route path="/reception/billing/:appointmentId" element={<ReceptionBillingDetailPage />} />
                        <Route path="/reception/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
                    </Route>
                </Route>
                <Route element={<RequireRole allowedRoles={[4]} />}>
                    <Route element={<AdminLayout />}>
                        <Route path="/admin" element={<Navigate to="/admin/dashboard" replace />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/dashboard" element={<AdminDashboardPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/configs" element={<AdminSystemConfigPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/doctors" element={<AdminDoctorsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/departments" element={<AdminDepartmentsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/medical-services" element={<AdminMedicalServicesPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/working-hours" element={<AdminWorkingHoursPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/users" element={<AdminUsersPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/exam-reports" element={<AdminExamReportsPage />} />
                        <Route path="/admin/profile" element={<ProfilePage />} />
                    </Route>
                </Route>
            </Routes>
        </BrowserRouter>
    );
}

export default App;

import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

import { getSpecialtyIconElement } from '../../utils/specialtyIcon';
import LocalImage from './LocalImage';

import './DepartmentCard.css';

function DepartmentCard({ department }) {
    return (
        <article className="department-card">
            <div className="department-card-media">
                <LocalImage
                    folder="departments"
                    name={department.name}
                    alt=""
                    fallback={getSpecialtyIconElement(department.name, { size: 52, strokeWidth: 1.3 })}
                />

                <span className="department-card-icon">{getSpecialtyIconElement(department.name, { size: 22 })}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="department-card-body">
                <h3>{department.name}</h3>

                <p>{department.description || 'Thông tin khám và tư vấn chuyên khoa.'}</p>

                <Link to={`/doctors?departmentId=${department.id}`} className="department-card-link">
                    Tìm bác sĩ
                    <ArrowRight size={16} />
                </Link>
            </div>
        </article>
    );
}

export default DepartmentCard;

===== ./src/components/common/DoctorCard.jsx =====
import { ArrowRight, UserRound } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

import LocalImage from './LocalImage';

import './DoctorCard.css';

function DoctorCard({ doctor, to, showBio = false }) {
    const experience =
        doctor.yearsExperience != null ? `${doctor.yearsExperience} năm kinh nghiệm` : 'Đang cập nhật kinh nghiệm';

    return (
        <article className="doctor-card">
            <div className="doctor-card-avatar">
                <LocalImage
                    folder="doctors"
                    name={doctor.fullName}
                    alt={doctor.fullName || ''}
                    fallback={<UserRound size={46} strokeWidth={1.5} />}
                />
            </div>

            <span className="doctor-card-department">{doctor.departmentName || 'Bác sĩ'}</span>

            <h3 className="doctor-card-name">{doctor.fullName || 'Chưa cập nhật tên bác sĩ'}</h3>

            <p className="doctor-card-experience">{experience}</p>

            {showBio && <p className="doctor-card-bio">{doctor.bio || 'Thông tin giới thiệu đang được cập nhật.'}</p>}

            <Link to={to} className="doctor-card-link">
                Đặt lịch khám
                <ArrowRight size={17} />
            </Link>
        </article>
    );
}

export default DoctorCard;

===== ./src/components/common/LocalImage.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';

import { toSlug } from '../../utils/slug';

const EXTENSIONS = ['jpg', 'png', 'webp'];

function ImageWithFallback({ folder, slug, alt, className, fallback }) {
    const [extensionIndex, setExtensionIndex] = useState(0);

    if (extensionIndex >= EXTENSIONS.length) {
        return fallback;
    }

    return (
        <img
            className={className}
            src={`/images/${folder}/${slug}.${EXTENSIONS[extensionIndex]}`}
            alt={alt}
            loading="lazy"
            onError={() => setExtensionIndex((index) => index + 1)}
        />
    );
}

// Ảnh nằm ở public/images/<folder>/<slug-của-name>.(jpg|png|webp).
// Không có ảnh thì hiện `fallback` (icon).
function LocalImage({ folder, name, alt = '', className, fallback = null }) {
    const slug = toSlug(name);

    if (!slug) {
        return fallback;
    }

    return (
        <ImageWithFallback
            key=***
            folder={folder}
            slug={slug}
            alt={alt}
            className={className}
            fallback={fallback}
        />
    );
}

export default LocalImage;

===== ./src/components/common/RedirectStaffToPortal.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';

import { apiGet } from '../../services/api';
import { unwrapData } from '../../utils/unwrap';
import { getRoleHome } from '../../utils/roleHome';

function RedirectStaffToPortal() {
    const { pathname } = useLocation();
    const [homePath, setHomePath] = useState(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function checkUser() {
            try {
                const user = unwrapData(await apiGet('/me'));
                if (cancelled) return;
                setHomePath(getRoleHome(Number(user?.role)));
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) setHomePath(null);
            }
        }

        checkUser();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, [pathname]);

    if (homePath) {
        return <Navigate to={homePath} replace />;
    }

    return <Outlet />;
}

export default RedirectStaffToPortal;

===== ./src/components/common/RequireRole.jsx =====
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { apiGet } from '../../services/api';
import { unwrapData } from '../../utils/unwrap';

function RequireRole({ allowedRoles }) {
    const location = useLocation();

    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function checkUser() {
            try {
                const response = await apiGet('/me');

                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(unwrapData(response));
                }
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(null);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setLoading(false);
                }
            }
        }

        checkUser();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="role-loading" role="status">
                Đang kiểm tra quyền truy cập...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!user) {
        return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
    }

    const role = Number(user.role);

    if (!allowedRoles.includes(role)) {
        if (role === 2) {
            return <Navigate to="/doctor/dashboard" replace />;
        }
        if (role === 3) {
            return <Navigate to="/reception/appointments" replace />;
        }
        if (role === 4) {
            return <Navigate to="/admin/dashboard" replace />;
        }
        return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
    }

    return <Outlet />;
}

export default RequireRole;

===== ./src/components/home/BookingSection.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { CalendarDays, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { getDepartments } from '../../services/departmentService';
import './BookingSection.css';

function BookingSection() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [departments, setDepartments] = useState([]);
    const [departmentId, setDepartmentId] = useState('');
    const [appointmentDate, setAppointmentDate] = useState('');

    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');

    const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA');

    useEffect(() => {
        getDepartments()
            .then((data) => {
                setDepartments(data);
            })
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error('Lỗi tải danh sách khoa:', err);
                setError('Không thể tải danh sách chuyên khoa.');
            })
            .finally(() => {
                setLoading(false);
            });
    }, []);

    function handleSearch(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        if (!departmentId || !appointmentDate) {
            return;
        }

        navigate(
            `/doctors?departmentId=${encodeURIComponent(departmentId)}&appointmentDate=${encodeURIComponent(appointmentDate)}`
        );
    }

    return (
        <section className="booking-section" id="booking">
            <div className="booking-heading">
                <span className="section-kicker">ĐẶT LỊCH TRỰC TUYẾN</span>
                <h2>Đặt lịch khám bệnh</h2>
                <p>Chọn chuyên khoa và ngày khám để bắt đầu tìm kiếm bác sĩ.</p>
            </div>

            <form className="booking-panel" onSubmit={handleSearch}>
                <label className="booking-field">
                    <span>Chuyên khoa</span>

                    <select
                        value={departmentId}
                        onChange={(event) => setDepartmentId(event.target.value)}
                        required
                        disabled={loading || Boolean(error)}
                    >
                        <option value="">{loading ? 'Đang tải chuyên khoa...' : 'Chọn chuyên khoa'}</option>

                        {departments.map((department) => (
                            <option key=***
                                {department.name}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </label>

                <label className="booking-field">
                    <span>Ngày khám dự kiến</span>

                    <div className="booking-date-input">
                        <CalendarDays size={18} aria-hidden="true" />

                        <input
                            type="date"
                            min={today}
                            value={appointmentDate}
                            onChange={(event) => setAppointmentDate(event.target.value)}
                            required
                        />
                    </div>
                </label>

                <button className="booking-submit" type="submit" disabled={loading || Boolean(error)}>
                    <Search size={18} />
                    Tìm bác sĩ
                </button>
            </form>

            {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}

            <p className="booking-note">Vui lòng chọn đúng chuyên khoa và ngày khám mong muốn.</p>
        </section>
    );
}

export default BookingSection;

===== ./src/components/home/DoctorShowcaseSection.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';

import DoctorCard from '../common/DoctorCard';
import { searchDoctors } from '../../services/doctorService';
import { unwrapList } from '../../utils/unwrap';

import './DoctorShowcaseSection.css';

const MAX_DOCTORS = 10;
const SKELETON_COUNT = 4;

function DoctorShowcaseSection() {
    const trackRef = useRef(null);
    const [doctors, setDoctors] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function loadDoctors() {
            try {
                const response = await searchDoctors('', '');
                if (cancelled) return;
                setDoctors(unwrapList(response).slice(0, MAX_DOCTORS));
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) setDoctors([]);
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        loadDoctors();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    const scrollTrack = (direction) => {
        const track = trackRef.current;
        if (!track) return;

        track.scrollBy({ left: direction * track.clientWidth * 0.8, behavior: 'smooth' });
    };

    if (!loading && doctors.length === 0) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <section className="doctor-showcase" aria-labelledby="doctor-showcase-title">
            <div className="container">
                <div className="doctor-showcase-header">
                    <div>
                        <span className="section-kicker">ĐỘI NGŨ BÁC SĨ</span>
                        <h2 id="doctor-showcase-title">Đặt khám bác sĩ</h2>
                        <p>Chọn bác sĩ phù hợp, xem khung giờ còn trống và đặt lịch ngay.</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="doctor-showcase-actions">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className="doctor-showcase-arrow"
                            aria-label="Xem bác sĩ trước"
                            onClick={() => scrollTrack(-1)}
                        >
                            <ChevronLeft size={20} />
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className="doctor-showcase-arrow"
                            aria-label="Xem bác sĩ tiếp theo"
                            onClick={() => scrollTrack(1)}
                        >
                            <ChevronRight size={20} />
                        </button>

                        <Link to="/doctors" className="doctor-showcase-all">
                            Xem tất cả
                            <ArrowRight size={17} />
                        </Link>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="doctor-showcase-track" ref={trackRef}>
                    {loading
                        ? Array.from({ length: SKELETON_COUNT }, (_, index) => (
                              <div className="doctor-showcase-skeleton" key=***
                          ))
                        : doctors.map((doctor) => (
                              <div className="doctor-showcase-item" key=***
                                  <DoctorCard doctor={doctor} to={`/doctors/${doctor.uuid}`} />
                              </div>
                          ))}
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

export default DoctorShowcaseSection;

===== ./src/components/home/SpecialtySection.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

import DepartmentCard from '../common/DepartmentCard';
import { getDepartments } from '../../services/departmentService';
import { unwrapList } from '../../utils/unwrap';

import './SpecialtySection.css';

const FEATURED_COUNT = 4;

function SpecialtySection() {
    const [specialties, setSpecialties] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function loadSpecialties() {
            try {
                const data = await getDepartments();
                if (cancelled) return;
                setSpecialties(unwrapList(data));
            } catch (err) {
                if (cancelled) return;
                console.error('Lỗi lấy danh sách khoa:', err);
                setError('Không thể tải danh sách chuyên khoa.');
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        loadSpecialties();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    return (
        <section className="specialty-section" id="specialties">
            <div className="container">
                <div className="specialty-heading">
                    <div>
                        <span className="section-kicker">CHUYÊN KHOA</span>
                        <h2>Chuyên khoa khám bệnh</h2>
                        <p>Lựa chọn chuyên khoa phù hợp với nhu cầu chăm sóc sức khỏe của bạn.</p>
                    </div>

                    <Link to="/departments" className="specialty-view-all">
                        Xem tất cả chuyên khoa
                        <ArrowRight size={17} />
                    </Link>
                </div>

                {!loading && error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}

                {(loading || !error) && (
                    <div className="specialty-grid">
                        {loading
                            ? Array.from({ length: FEATURED_COUNT }, (_, index) => (
                                  <div className="specialty-skeleton" key=***
                              ))
                            : specialties
                                  .slice(0, FEATURED_COUNT)
                                  .map((department) => <DepartmentCard department={department} key=***
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

export default SpecialtySection;

===== ./src/components/home/StepsSection.jsx =====
import { Building2, UserRound, CalendarDays, ClipboardCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import './StepsSection.css';

const steps = [
    {
        id: 1,
        title: 'Chọn chuyên khoa',
        description: 'Tìm chuyên khoa phù hợp với nhu cầu khám và tư vấn sức khỏe.',
        icon: Building2,
    },
    {
        id: 2,

        icon: UserRound,
    },
    {
        id: 3,
        title: 'Chọn lịch khám',
        description: 'Chọn ngày khám và khung giờ còn trống của bác sĩ.',
        icon: CalendarDays,
    },
    {
        id: 4,
        title: 'Xác nhận đặt lịch',
        description: 'Kiểm tra thông tin và gửi yêu cầu đặt lịch khám.',
        icon: ClipboardCheck,
    },
];

function StepsSection() {
    return (
        <section className="steps-section" id="booking-guide">
            <div className="container">
                <div className="steps-heading">
                    <span className="eyebrow">QUY TRÌNH ĐẶT LỊCH</span>

                    <h2>Đặt lịch khám đơn giản trong 4 bước</h2>

                    <p>Chủ động lựa chọn lịch khám phù hợp với thời gian của bạn.</p>
                </div>

                <div className="steps-grid">
                    {steps.map((step) => {
                        const Icon = step.icon;

                        return (
                            <article className="step-item" key=***
                                <div className="step-icon-wrapper">
                                    <div className="step-icon">
                                        <Icon size={25} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                                    </div>

                                    <span className="step-number">{String(step.id).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
                                </div>

                                <h3>{step.title}</h3>

                                <p>{step.description}</p>
                            </article>
                        );
                    })}
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

export default StepsSection;

===== ./src/components/layout/Header.jsx =====
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link, NavLink, useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    Plus,
    Menu,
    X,
    User,
    UserRound,
    LogOut,
    CalendarDays,
    ChevronDown,
    ClipboardCheck,
    ReceiptText,
} from 'lucide-react';

import { apiGet, apiPost } from '../../services/api';
import './Header.css';

function Header() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const location = useLocation();

    const [isHidden, setIsHidden] = useState(false);
    const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isBookingMenuOpen, setIsBookingMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
    const [isAuthLoading, setIsAuthLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function fetchCurrentUser() {
            try {
                const currentUser = await apiGet('/me');

                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(currentUser);
                }
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(null);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setIsAuthLoading(false);
                }
            }
        }

        fetchCurrentUser();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, [location.pathname]);

    useEffect(() => {
        let lastScrollY = window.scrollY;

        const handleScroll = () => {
            const currentScrollY = window.scrollY;

            if (currentScrollY <= 80) {
                setIsHidden(false);
            } else if (currentScrollY > lastScrollY) {
                setIsHidden(true);
                setIsMenuOpen(false);
                setIsBookingMenuOpen(false);
            } else {
                setIsHidden(false);
            }

            lastScrollY = currentScrollY;
        };

        window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });

        return () => {
            window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
        };
    }, []);

    const closeMenu = () => {
        setIsMenuOpen(false);
        setIsBookingMenuOpen(false);
    };

    const handleLogout = async () => {
        try {
            await apiPost('/auth/logout', {});
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Lỗi đăng xuất:', error);
        } finally {
            setUser(null);
            closeMenu();
            navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <header className={`site-header ${isHidden ? 'header-hidden' : ''}`}>
            <div className="header-inner">
                {/* Logo */}
                <Link to="/" className="brand" onClick={closeMenu}>
                    <span className="brand-mark">
                        <Plus size={30} strokeWidth={3} />
                    </span>

                    <span className="brand-text">
                        <strong>BỆNH VIỆN NAM LƯƠNG SƠN</strong>
                        <small>Chăm sóc tận tâm – Sức khỏe vẹn toàn</small>
                    </span>
                </Link>

                <nav className="main-nav" aria-label="Điều hướng chính">
                    <NavLink to="/" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Trang chủ
                    </NavLink>

                    <NavLink to="/doctors" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Đội ngũ bác sĩ
                    </NavLink>

                    <NavLink to="/departments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Chuyên khoa
                    </NavLink>

                    <div className="booking-dropdown">
                        <div className="booking-dropdown-trigger">
                            <NavLink to="/appointments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                Đặt lịch khám
                            </NavLink>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                className="booking-dropdown-toggle"
                                aria-label="Mở menu lịch khám"
                                aria-expanded={isBookingMenuOpen}
                                onClick={() => setIsBookingMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
                            >
                                <ChevronDown size={15} />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className={`booking-dropdown-menu ${isBookingMenuOpen ? 'dropdown-open' : ''}`}>
                            {user && !isAuthLoading && (
                                <>
                                    <NavLink to="/profile" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                        <UserRound size={17} />
                                        <span>Hồ sơ cá nhân</span>
                                    </NavLink>
                                    <NavLink to="/my-appointments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                        <CalendarDays size={17} />
                                        <span>Lịch khám của tôi</span>
                                    </NavLink>

                                    <NavLink to="/my-exam-reports" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                        <ClipboardCheck size={17} />
                                        <span>Kết quả khám của tôi</span>
                                    </NavLink>

                                    <NavLink to="/my-payments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                        <ReceiptText size={17} />
                                        <span>Lịch sử thanh toán</span>
                                    </NavLink>
                                </>
                            )}

                            <NavLink to="/appointments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                <Plus size={17} />
                                <span>Đặt lịch khám mới</span>
                            </NavLink>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <NavLink to="/medical-services" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Dịch vụ y tế
                    </NavLink>
                </nav>

                <div className="header-account">
                    {isAuthLoading ? (
                        <div className="account-loading" aria-label="Đang kiểm tra đăng nhập" />
                    ) : user ? (
                        <>
                            <span className="header-user">
                                <User size={16} />
                                <span>{user.fullName || 'Tài khoản'}</span>
                            </span>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleLogout}
                                className="header-login header-logout"
                                title="Đăng xuất"
                            >
                                <LogOut size={16} />
                                <span>Đăng xuất</span>
                            </button>
                        </>
                    ) : (
                        <Link to="/login" className="header-login" onClick={closeMenu}>
                            Đăng nhập
                        </Link>
                    )}
                </div>

                <button
                    type="button"
                    className="mobile-menu"
                    aria-label={isMenuOpen ? 'Đóng menu' : 'Mở menu'}
                    aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
                    onClick={() => {
                        setIsMenuOpen((open) => !open);
                        setIsBookingMenuOpen(false);
                    }}
                >
                    {isMenuOpen ? <X size={23} /> : <Menu size={23} />}
                </button>
            </div>

            {isMenuOpen && (
                <nav className="mobile-nav" aria-label="Điều hướng di động">
                    <NavLink to="/" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Trang chủ
                    </NavLink>

                    <NavLink to="/doctors" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Đội ngũ bác sĩ
                    </NavLink>

                    <NavLink to="/departments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Chuyên khoa
                    </NavLink>

                    <NavLink to="/appointments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Đặt lịch khám
                    </NavLink>

                    {!isAuthLoading && user && (
                        <div className="mobile-booking-submenu">
                            <NavLink to="/profile" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                <UserRound size={17} />
                                Hồ sơ cá nhân
                            </NavLink>

                            <NavLink to="/my-appointments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                <CalendarDays size={17} />
                                Lịch khám của tôi
                            </NavLink>

                            <NavLink to="/my-exam-reports" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                <ClipboardCheck size={17} />
                                Kết quả khám của tôi
                            </NavLink>

                            <NavLink to="/my-payments" onClick={closeMenu}>
                                <ReceiptText size={17} />
                                Lịch sử thanh toán
                            </NavLink>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <NavLink to="/medical-services" onClick={closeMenu}>
                        Dịch vụ y tế
                    </NavLink>

                    {isAuthLoading ? null : user ? (
                        <button type="button" className="mobile-logout" onClick={handleLogout}>
                            <LogOut size={18} />
                            Đăng xuất
                        </button>
                    ) : (
                        <NavLink to="/login" onClick={closeMenu}>
                            Đăng nhập
                        </NavLink>
                    )}
                </nav>
            )}
        </header>
    );
}

export default Header;

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

import {
    Activity,
    Building2,
    ClipboardList,
    Clock3,
    FileText,
    LayoutDashboard,
    LogOut,
    Menu,
    Settings,
    ShieldCheck,
    Stethoscope,
    Users,
    X,
    UserRound,
} from 'lucide-react';

import { apiGet, apiPost } from '../services/api';
import { unwrapData } from '../utils/unwrap';
import './AdminLayout.css';

const navItems = [
    { to: '/admin/dashboard', label: 'Thống kê', icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { to: '/admin/users', label: 'Tài khoản', icon: Users },
    { to: '/admin/doctors', label: 'Bác sĩ', icon: Stethoscope },
    { to: '/admin/departments', label: 'Chuyên khoa', icon: Building2 },
    { to: '/admin/medical-services', label: 'Dịch vụ y tế', icon: ClipboardList },
    { to: '/admin/working-hours', label: 'Lịch làm việc', icon: Clock3 },
    { to: '/admin/exam-reports', label: 'Phiếu khám', icon: FileText },
    { to: '/admin/configs', label: 'Cấu hình hệ thống', icon: Settings },
    { to: '/admin/profile', label: 'Hồ sơ cá nhân', icon: UserRound },
];

function AdminLayout() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
    const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function fetchCurrentUser() {
            try {
                const response = await apiGet('/me');

                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(unwrapData(response));
                }
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(null);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setIsLoading(false);
                }
            }
        }

        fetchCurrentUser();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    const closeMenu = () => setIsMenuOpen(false);

    const handleLogout = async () => {
        try {
            await apiPost('/auth/logout', {});
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Lỗi đăng xuất:', error);
        } finally {
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            setUser(null);
            navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="admin-layout">
            <aside className={`admin-sidebar ${isMenuOpen ? 'admin-sidebar-open' : ''}`}>
                <NavLink to="/admin/dashboard" className="admin-brand" onClick={closeMenu}>
                    <span className="admin-brand-icon">
                        <ShieldCheck size={25} />
                    </span>

                    <span className="admin-brand-text">
                        <strong>NAM LƯƠNG SƠN</strong>
                        <small>Khu vực quản trị</small>
                    </span>
                </NavLink>

                <div className="admin-sidebar-section">
                    <span className="admin-sidebar-heading">Quản trị</span>

                    <nav className="admin-nav" aria-label="Điều hướng quản trị">
                        {navItems.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon }) => (
                            <NavLink
                                key=***
                                to={to}
                                onClick={closeMenu}
                                className={({ isActive }) =>
                                    `admin-nav-link ${isActive ? 'admin-nav-link-active' : ''}`
                                }
                            >
                                <Icon size={19} />
                                <span>{label}</span>
                            </NavLink>
                        ))}
                    </nav>
                </div>
            </aside>

            {isMenuOpen && (
                <button type="button" className="admin-sidebar-overlay" aria-label="Đóng menu" onClick={closeMenu} />
            )}

            <div className="admin-main">
                <header className="admin-topbar">
                    <div className="admin-topbar-left">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className="admin-mobile-menu"
                            aria-label={isMenuOpen ? 'Đóng menu' : 'Mở menu'}
                            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
                        >
                            {isMenuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
                        </button>

                        <div className="admin-topbar-title">
                            <span>Bệnh viện Nam Lương Sơn</span>
                            <strong>Cổng quản trị</strong>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="admin-account">
                        <div className="admin-account-avatar">
                            <ShieldCheck size={20} />
                        </div>

                        <div className="admin-account-info">
                            <strong>{isLoading ? 'Đang tải...' : user?.fullName || 'Tài khoản quản trị'}</strong>
                            <span>Quản trị viên</span>
                        </div>

                        <button type="button" className="admin-logout-button" onClick={handleLogout} title="Đăng xuất">
                            <LogOut size={18} />
                            <span>Đăng xuất</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </header>

                <main className="admin-content">
                    <Outlet />
                </main>

                <footer className="admin-footer">
                    <Activity size={15} />
                    <span>Hệ thống quản lý khám bệnh · Bệnh viện Nam Lương Sơn</span>
                </footer>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

export default AdminLayout;

===== ./src/layouts/DoctorLayout.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    Activity,
    CalendarDays,
    ClipboardList,
    Clock3,
    LayoutDashboard,
    LogOut,
    Menu,
    Stethoscope,
    X,
    UserRound,
} from 'lucide-react';

import { apiGet, apiPost } from '../services/api';
import { unwrapData } from '../utils/unwrap';
import './StaffLayout.css';

function DoctorLayout() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
    const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function fetchCurrentUser() {
            try {
                const response = await apiGet('/me');

                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(unwrapData(response));
                }
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(null);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setIsLoading(false);
                }
            }
        }

        fetchCurrentUser();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    const closeMenu = () => setIsMenuOpen(false);

    const handleLogout = async () => {
        try {
            await apiPost('/auth/logout', {});
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Lỗi đăng xuất:', error);
        } finally {
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            setUser(null);
            navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
    };

    const navItems = [
        {
            to: '/doctor/dashboard',

            end: true,
        },
        {
            to: '/doctor/appointments',

        },
        {
            to: '/doctor/schedule',

        {
            to: '/doctor/exam-reports',

            icon: ClipboardList,
        },
        {
            to: '/doctor/profile',
            label: 'Hồ sơ cá nhân',
            icon: UserRound,
        },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="doctor-layout">
            <aside className={`doctor-sidebar ${isMenuOpen ? 'doctor-sidebar-open' : ''}`}>
                <NavLink to="/doctor/dashboard" className="doctor-brand" onClick={closeMenu}>
                    <span className="doctor-brand-icon">
                        <Stethoscope size={25} />
                    </span>

                    <span className="doctor-brand-text">
                        <strong>NAM LƯƠNG SƠN</strong>
                        <small>CỔNG THÔNG TIN BÁC SĨ</small>
                    </span>
                </NavLink>

                <div className="doctor-sidebar-section">
                    <span className="doctor-sidebar-heading">KHÔNG GIAN LÀM VIỆC</span>

                    <nav className="doctor-nav" aria-label="Điều hướng bác sĩ">
                        {navItems.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon, end }) => (
                            <NavLink
                                key=***
                                to={to}
                                end={end}
                                onClick={closeMenu}
                                className={({ isActive }) =>
                                    `doctor-nav-link ${isActive ? 'doctor-nav-link-active' : ''}`
                                }
                            >
                                <Icon size={19} />
                                <span>{label}</span>
                            </NavLink>
                        ))}
                    </nav>
                </div>

                <div className="doctor-sidebar-footer">
                    <div className="doctor-sidebar-footer-icon">
                        <Stethoscope size={20} />
                    </div>

    );

===== ./src/layouts/PublicLayout.jsx =====
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom';
import Header from '../components/layout/Header';
import Footer from '../components/layout/Footer';

function PublicLayout() {
    return (
        <div className="site-layout">
            <Header />
            <main className="site-main">
                <Outlet />
            </main>
            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}

export default PublicLayout;

===== ./src/layouts/ReceptionLayout.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Activity, CalendarDays, LogOut, Menu, Receipt, Users, X, UserRound } from 'lucide-react';

import { apiGet, apiPost } from '../services/api';
import { unwrapData } from '../utils/unwrap';
import './StaffLayout.css';

const NAV_ITEMS = [
    {
        to: '/reception/appointments',
        label: 'Lịch hẹn',
        icon: CalendarDays,
    },
    {
        to: '/reception/patients',
        label: 'Bệnh nhân',
        icon: Users,
    },
    {
        to: '/reception/billing',
        label: 'Thu ngân',
        icon: Receipt,
    },
    {
        to: '/reception/profile',
        label: 'Hồ sơ cá nhân',
        icon: UserRound,
    },
];

function ReceptionLayout() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
    const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function fetchCurrentUser() {
            try {
                const response = await apiGet('/me');

                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(unwrapData(response));
                }
            } catch {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setUser(null);
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setIsLoading(false);
                }
            }
        }

        fetchCurrentUser();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    const closeMenu = () => setIsMenuOpen(false);

    const handleLogout = async () => {
        try {
            await apiPost('/auth/logout', {});
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Lỗi đăng xuất:', error);
        } finally {
            localStorage.removeItem('user');
            setUser(null);
            navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="doctor-layout">
            <aside className={`doctor-sidebar ${isMenuOpen ? 'doctor-sidebar-open' : ''}`}>
                <NavLink to="/reception/appointments" className="doctor-brand" onClick={closeMenu}>
                    <span className="doctor-brand-icon">
                        <Activity size={25} />
                    </span>

                    <span className="doctor-brand-text">
                        <strong>NAM LƯƠNG SƠN</strong>
                        <small>CỔNG THÔNG TIN LỄ TÂN</small>
                    </span>
                </NavLink>

                <div className="doctor-sidebar-section">
                    <span className="doctor-sidebar-heading">QUẦY TIẾP ĐÓN</span>

                    <nav className="doctor-nav" aria-label="Điều hướng lễ tân">
                        {NAV_ITEMS.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon }) => (
                            <NavLink
                                key=***
                                to={to}
                                onClick={closeMenu}
                                className={({ isActive }) =>
                                    `doctor-nav-link ${isActive ? 'doctor-nav-link-active' : ''}`
                                }
                            >
                                <Icon size={19} />
                                <span>{label}</span>
                            </NavLink>
                        ))}
                    </nav>
                </div>

                <div className="doctor-sidebar-footer">
                    <div className="doctor-sidebar-footer-icon">
                        <Users size={20} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <strong>Hệ thống lễ tân</strong>
                        <span>Tiếp đón và thu ngân</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </aside>

            {isMenuOpen && (
                <button type="button" className="doctor-sidebar-overlay" aria-label="Đóng menu" onClick={closeMenu} />
            )}

            <div className="doctor-main">
                <header className="doctor-topbar">
                    <div className="doctor-topbar-left">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className="doctor-mobile-menu"
                            aria-label={isMenuOpen ? 'Đóng menu' : 'Mở menu'}
                            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
                        >
                            {isMenuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
                        </button>

                        <div className="doctor-topbar-title">
                            <span className="doctor-topbar-eyebrow">BỆNH VIỆN NAM LƯƠNG SƠN</span>
                            <strong>Cổng làm việc lễ tân</strong>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="doctor-account">
                        <div className="doctor-account-avatar">
                            <Users size={20} />
                        </div>

                        <div className="doctor-account-info">
                            <strong>{isLoading ? 'Đang tải...' : user?.fullName || 'Tài khoản lễ tân'}</strong>
                            <span>Lễ tân</span>
                        </div>

                        <button type="button" className="doctor-logout-button" onClick={handleLogout} title="Đăng xuất">
                            <LogOut size={18} />
                            <span>Đăng xuất</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </header>

                <main className="doctor-content">
                    <Outlet />
                </main>

                <footer className="doctor-footer">
                    <Activity size={15} />
                    <span>Hệ thống quản lý khám bệnh · Bệnh viện Nam Lương Sơn</span>
                </footer>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

export default ReceptionLayout;

===== ./src/pages/account/ChangePasswordCard.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Lock, LoaderCircle, Save } from 'lucide-react';

import { changePassword } from '../../services/profileService';

import './ProfilePage.css';

const EMPTY_FORM = { currentPassword: ***

function validateForm(form) {
    if (!form.currentPassword) return 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu hiện tại.';
    if (form.newPassword.length < 8 || form.newPassword.length > 72) {
        return 'Mật khẩu mới phải từ 8 đến 72 ký tự.';
    }
    if (form.newPassword === form.currentPassword) return 'Mật khẩu mới phải khác mật khẩu hiện tại.';
    if (form.confirmPassword !== form.newPassword) return 'Xác nhận mật khẩu mới không khớp.';
    return '';
}

function ChangePasswordCard() {
    const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState('');

    function handleChange(event) {
        const { name, value } = event.target;

        setForm((previous) => ({ ...previous, [name]: value }));
        setError('');
        setSuccess('');
    }

    async function handleSubmit(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        if (saving) return;

        const message = validateForm(form);
        if (message) {
            setError(message);
            return;
        }

        setSaving(true);
        setError('');
        setSuccess('');

        try {
            await changePassword({
                currentPassword: ***
                newPassword: ***
            });

            setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
            setSuccess('Đổi mật khẩu thành công.');
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err?.message || 'Không thể đổi mật khẩu. Vui lòng thử lại.');
        } finally {
            setSaving(false);
        }
    }

    const inputType = showPassword ? 'text' : 'password';

    return (
        <div className="profile-card" style={{ marginTop: 24 }}>
            <div className="profile-card-heading">
                <div>
                    <h2>Đổi mật khẩu</h2>
                    <p>Nhập mật khẩu hiện tại để đặt mật khẩu mới.</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
                {error && (
                    <div className="profile-alert profile-alert-error" role="alert">
                        <AlertCircle size={19} />
                        <span>{error}</span>
                    </div>
                )}

                {success && (
                    <div className="profile-alert profile-alert-success" role="status">
                        <CheckCircle2 size={19} />
                        <span>{success}</span>
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className="profile-form-grid">
                    <div className="profile-field">
                        <label htmlFor="password-current">
                            Mật khẩu hiện tại
                            <span className="profile-required">*</span>
                        </label>

                        <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                            <Lock size={18} />
                            <input
                                id="password-current"
                                name="currentPassword"
                                type={inputType}
                                autoComplete="current-password"
                                value={form.currentPassword}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                disabled={saving}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="profile-field">
                        <label htmlFor="password-new">
                            Mật khẩu mới
                            <span className="profile-required">*</span>
                        </label>

                        <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                            <Lock size={18} />
                            <input
                                id="password-new"
                                name="newPassword"
                                type={inputType}
                                autoComplete="new-password"
                                maxLength={72}
                                value={form.newPassword}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                disabled={saving}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <small className="profile-field-note">Từ 8 đến 72 ký tự.</small>
                    </div>

                    <div className="profile-field">
                        <label htmlFor="password-confirm">
                            Nhập lại mật khẩu mới
                            <span className="profile-required">*</span>
                        </label>

                        <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                            <Lock size={18} />
                            <input
                                id="password-confirm"
                                name="confirmPassword"
                                type={inputType}
                                autoComplete="new-password"
                                maxLength={72}
                                value={form.confirmPassword}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                disabled={saving}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="profile-form-footer">
                    <label className="profile-hint" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                        <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={showPassword}
                            onChange={(event) => setShowPassword(event.target.checked)}
                        />
                        Hiển thị mật khẩu
                    </label>

                    <button type="submit" className="profile-button" disabled={saving}>
                        {saving ? <LoaderCircle size={18} className="profile-spinner" /> : <Save size={18} />}
                        {saving ? 'Đang lưu...' : 'Đổi mật khẩu'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
}

export default ChangePasswordCard;

===== ./src/pages/account/ProfilePage.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
    UserRound,
    Mail,
    Phone,
    CalendarDays,
    Save,
    RefreshCw,
    AlertCircle,
    CheckCircle2,
    LoaderCircle,
    ShieldCheck,
    Lock,
} from 'lucide-react';

import { getMyProfile, updateMyProfile } from '../../services/profileService';
import ChangePasswordCard from './ChangePasswordCard';

import './ProfilePage.css';

const EMPTY_FORM = {
    fullName: '',
    phone: '',
    email: '',
    dateOfBirth: '',
};

function toFormData(profile) {
    return {
        fullName: profile?.fullName ?? '',
        phone: profile?.phone ?? '',
        email: profile?.email ?? '',
        dateOfBirth: profile?.dateOfBirth ? String(profile.dateOfBirth).slice(0, 10) : '',
    };
}

function getErrorMessage(error) {
    return error?.message || 'Đã xảy ra lỗi. Vui lòng thử lại.';
}

function ProfilePage() {
    const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    const [pageError, setPageError] = useState('');
    const [formError, setFormError] = useState('');
    const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState('');
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('profile'); // 'profile' hoặc 'password'

    const loadProfile = useCallback(async () => {
        setLoading(true);
        setPageError('');
        setFormError('');
        setSuccessMessage('');

        try {
            const profile = await getMyProfile();
            setForm(toFormData(profile));
        } catch (error) {
            setPageError(getErrorMessage(error));
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function fetchProfile() {
            try {
                const profile = await getMyProfile();
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setForm(toFormData(profile));
                }
            } catch (error) {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setPageError(getErrorMessage(error));
                }
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    setLoading(false);
                }
            }
        }

        fetchProfile();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, []);

    function handleChange(event) {
        const { name, value } = event.target;
        setForm((previous) => ({
            ...previous,
            [name]: value,
        }));
        setFormError('');
        setSuccessMessage('');
    }

    async function handleSubmit(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        setFormError('');
        setSuccessMessage('');

        const fullName = form.fullName.trim();
        const phone = form.phone.trim();

        if (!fullName) {
            setFormError('Vui lòng nhập họ và tên.');
            return;
        }

        if (fullName.length > 150) {
            setFormError('Họ và tên không được vượt quá 150 ký tự.');
            return;
        }

        if (!phone) {
            setFormError('Vui lòng nhập số điện thoại.');
            return;
        }

        if (!/^(0|\+84)\d{9}$/.test(phone)) {
            setFormError('Số điện thoại không đúng định dạng. Ví dụ: 0912345678.');
            return;
        }

        if (form.dateOfBirth) {
            const today = new Date();
            const selectedDate = new Date(`${form.dateOfBirth}T00:00:00`);
            const todayString = [
                today.getFullYear(),
                String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
                String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'),
            ].join('-');

            if (Number.isNaN(selectedDate.getTime()) || form.dateOfBirth >= todayString) {
                setFormError('Ngày sinh phải nằm trong quá khứ.');
                return;
            }
        }

        setSaving(true);

        try {
            const updatedProfile = await updateMyProfile({
                fullName,
                phone,
                dateOfBirth: form.dateOfBirth || null,
            });

            setForm(toFormData(updatedProfile));
            setSuccessMessage('Cập nhật hồ sơ thành công.');
        } catch (error) {
            setFormError(getErrorMessage(error));
        } finally {
            setSaving(false);
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <section className="profile-page">
                <div className="profile-state">
                    <LoaderCircle className="profile-spinner" size={32} />
                    <p>Đang tải thông tin hồ sơ...</p>
                </div>
            </section>
        );
    }

    if (pageError) {
        return (
            <section className="profile-page">
                <div className="profile-state profile-state-error">
                    <AlertCircle size={36} />
                    <h2>Không thể tải hồ sơ</h2>
                    <p>{pageError}</p>
                    <button type="button" className="profile-button" onClick={loadProfile}>
                        <RefreshCw size={17} />
                        Thử lại
                    </button>
                </div>
            </section>
        );
    }

    return (
        <section className="profile-page">
            <div className="profile-container">
                {/* Header Tổng quan */}
                <header className="profile-hero">
                    <div className="profile-hero-user">
                        <div className="profile-hero-avatar">
                            <UserRound size={36} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="profile-hero-badge">
                                <ShieldCheck size={14} /> Tài khoản bệnh nhân
                            </div>
                            <h1>{form.fullName || 'Hồ sơ cá nhân'}</h1>
                            <p>Quản lý thông tin bảo mật và hồ sơ y tế tại Bệnh viện Nam Lương Sơn.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </header>

                {/* Bố cục dạng Dashboard 2 cột (Sidebar + Content) */}
                <div className="profile-layout">
                    {/* Cột Menu bên trái */}
                    <aside className="profile-sidebar">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className={`profile-nav-item ${activeTab === 'profile' ? 'active' : ''}`}
                            onClick={() => setActiveTab('profile')}
                        >
                            <UserRound size={18} />
                            <span>Thông tin cá nhân</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className={`profile-nav-item ${activeTab === 'password' ? 'active' : ''}`}
                            onClick={() => setActiveTab('password')}
                        >
                            <Lock size={18} />
                            <span>Đổi mật khẩu</span>
                        </button>
                    </aside>

                    {/* Cột Nội dung chính bên phải */}
                    <main className="profile-main-content">
                        {activeTab === 'profile' ? (
                            <div className="profile-card">
                                <div className="profile-card-heading">
                                    <h2>Thông tin cá nhân</h2>
                                    <p>
                                        Vui lòng đảm bảo thông tin số điện thoại và họ tên luôn chính xác để thuận tiện
                                        đặt lịch.
                                    </p>
                                </div>

                                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
                                    {formError && (
                                        <div className="profile-alert profile-alert-error" role="alert">
                                            <AlertCircle size={19} />
                                            <span>{formError}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    )}

                                    {successMessage && (
                                        <div className="profile-alert profile-alert-success" role="status">
                                            <CheckCircle2 size={19} />
                                            <span>{successMessage}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    )}

                                    <div className="profile-form-grid">
                                        <div className="profile-field">
                                            <label htmlFor="profile-fullName">
                                                Họ và tên <span className="profile-required">*</span>
                                            </label>
                                            <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                                                <UserRound size={18} />
                                                <input
                                                    id="profile-fullName"
                                                    name="fullName"
                                                    type="text"
                                                    autoComplete="name"
                                                    maxLength={150}
                                                    value={form.fullName}
                                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                                    placeholder="Nhập họ và tên"
                                                    required
                                                />
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="profile-field">
                                            <label htmlFor="profile-phone">
                                                Số điện thoại <span className="profile-required">*</span>
                                            </label>
                                            <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                                                <Phone size={18} />
                                                <input
                                                    id="profile-phone"
                                                    name="phone"
                                                    type="tel"
                                                    autoComplete="tel"
                                                    value={form.phone}
                                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                                    placeholder="Ví dụ: 0912345678"
                                                    required
                                                />
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="profile-field">
                                            <label htmlFor="profile-email">Email đăng nhập</label>
                                            <div className="profile-input-wrap profile-input-readonly">
                                                <Mail size={18} />
                                                <input
                                                    id="profile-email"
                                                    name="email"
                                                    type="email"
                                                    value={form.email}
                                                    readOnly
                                                />
                                            </div>
                                            <small className="profile-field-note">
                                                Email dùng để định danh và không thể thay đổi.
                                            </small>
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="profile-field">
                                            <label htmlFor="profile-dateOfBirth">Ngày sinh</label>
                                            <div className="profile-input-wrap">
                                                <CalendarDays size={18} />
                                                <input
                                                    id="profile-dateOfBirth"
                                                    name="dateOfBirth"
                                                    type="date"
                                                    value={form.dateOfBirth}
                                                    max={new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA')}
                                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                                />
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="profile-form-footer">
                                        <p className="profile-hint">
                                            <span className="profile-required">*</span> Thông tin bắt buộc điền.
                                        </p>
                                        <button type="submit" className="profile-button" disabled={saving}>
                                            {saving ? (
                                                <LoaderCircle size={18} className="profile-spinner" />
                                            ) : (
                                                <Save size={18} />
                                            )}
                                            {saving ? 'Đang lưu...' : 'Lưu thay đổi'}
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </form>
                            </div>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="profile-card password-card-wrapper">
                                <ChangePasswordCard />
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div className="profile-security-note">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
                            <p>
                                Dữ liệu cá nhân của bạn được mã hóa và bảo mật nghiêm ngặt theo quy định của Bệnh viện
                                Nam Lương Sơn.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </main>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

export default ProfilePage;

===== ./src/pages/admin/AdminDashboardPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { CalendarDays, CheckCircle2, Percent, RefreshCw, UserX, Wallet, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';

import { getAppointmentStatistics, getRevenueStatistics, getTopDoctors } from '../../services/statisticsService';
import { unwrapData, unwrapList } from '../../utils/unwrap';

import './AdminPage.css';

function toDateString(date) {
    const year = date.getFullYear();
    const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

function getMonthRange() {
    const today = new Date();
    return {
        from: toDateString(new Date(today.getFullYear(), today.getMonth(), 1)),
        to: toDateString(today),
    };
}

function getLastDaysRange(days) {
    const today = new Date();
    const start = new Date(today);
    start.setDate(today.getDate() - (days - 1));
    return { from: toDateString(start), to: toDateString(today) };
}

const formatNumber = (value) => Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('vi-VN');
const formatMoney = (value) => `${formatNumber(value)} ₫`;

function AdminDashboardPage() {
    const [range, setRange] = useState(getMonthRange);
    const [appliedRange, setAppliedRange] = useState(getMonthRange);
    const [data, setData] = useState(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false;

        async function loadStatistics() {
            try {
                const [appointmentResponse, revenueResponse, topDoctorResponse] = await Promise.all([
                    getAppointmentStatistics(appliedRange.from, appliedRange.to),
                    getRevenueStatistics(appliedRange.from, appliedRange.to),
                    getTopDoctors(appliedRange.from, appliedRange.to),
                ]);

                if (cancelled) return;

                setData({
                    appointments: unwrapData(appointmentResponse) || {},
                    revenue: unwrapData(revenueResponse) || {},
                    topDoctors: unwrapList(topDoctorResponse),
                });
                setError('');
            } catch (err) {
                if (cancelled) return;
                setData(null);
                setError(err.message || 'Không thể tải số liệu thống kê.');
            } finally {
                if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        loadStatistics();

        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, [appliedRange]);

    const applyRange = (nextRange) => {
        if (!nextRange.from || !nextRange.to) {
            setError('Hãy chọn đủ ngày bắt đầu và ngày kết thúc.');
            return;
        }

        if (nextRange.from > nextRange.to) {
            setError('Ngày bắt đầu phải trước hoặc cùng ngày kết thúc.');
            return;
        }

        setRange(nextRange);
        setError('');
        setLoading(true);
        setAppliedRange({ ...nextRange });
    };

    const handleSubmit = (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        applyRange(range);
    };

    const appointments = data?.appointments;
    const total = Number(appointments?.totalAppointments || 0);
    const cancelled = Number(appointments?.cancelledAppointments || 0);
    // Tự tính tỷ lệ hủy từ số lượt để không phụ thuộc đơn vị của cancellationRate
    const cancelRate = total > 0 ? (cancelled / total) * 100 : 0;

    const show = (value) => {
        if (loading) return '…';
        if (!data) return '—';
        return value;
    };

    const topDoctors = data?.topDoctors || [];
    const maxCompleted = topDoctors.reduce((max, item) => Math.max(max, Number(item.completedAppointments || 0)), 0);

    return (
        <section className="admin-page">
            <div className="admin-page__heading">
                <div>
                    <h1>Thống kê hoạt động</h1>
                    <p>Số liệu lịch khám, doanh thu và bác sĩ nhiều lượt khám theo khoảng ngày.</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <form className="admin-toolbar" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
                <label className="admin-field">
                    <span>Từ ngày</span>
                    <input
                        type="date"
                        value={range.from}
                        max={range.to || undefined}
                        onChange={(event) => setRange((prev) => ({ ...prev, from: event.target.value }))}
                    />
                </label>

                <label className="admin-field">
                    <span>Đến ngày</span>
                    <input
                        type="date"
                        value={range.to}
                        min={range.from || undefined}
                        onChange={(event) => setRange((prev) => ({ ...prev, to: event.target.value }))}
                    />
                </label>

                <button type="submit" className="admin-btn admin-btn-primary" disabled={loading}>
                    <RefreshCw size={16} />
                    Xem thống kê
                </button>

                <div className="admin-toolbar__quick">
                    <button type="button" className="admin-btn" onClick={() => applyRange(getMonthRange())}>
                        Tháng này
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" className="admin-btn" onClick={() => applyRange(getLastDaysRange(7))}>
                        7 ngày qua
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" className="admin-btn" onClick={() => applyRange(getLastDaysRange(30))}>
                        30 ngày qua
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>

            {error && (
                <p className="admin-alert admin-alert-error" role="alert">
                    {error}
